([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/peak-annotation-with-bounding-boxes))
- **peak-apex-identification-from-intensity-profiles**: Use when after EIC candidate generation and peak detection have been completed on LC/HRMS data, when you need to extract the retention time and intensity values at peak maxima for each detected peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/peak-apex-identification-from-intensity-profiles))
- **peak-area-and-intensity-measurement**: Use when when you have vendor-format GC-CI-MS or LC-MS data from stable isotope labeling experiments, a target list of compounds with known monoisotopic m/z, retention time, and elemental formula, and you need per-isotopologue area and intensity values for quantification or downstream statistical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/peak-area-and-intensity-measurement) · 2 copies)
- **peak-area-correlation-analysis**: Use when a matched feature table with peak areas from PeakBot and XCMS (e.g. from a PHM tool comparison) must be compared: regression R2, trimming of dissimilar features, and derivation of the profile-to-centroid median area factor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-area-correlation-analysis))
- **peak-area-integration**: Use when processing AEMS MRM raw data (XIC points per mass transition and ejection/well) to produce a baseline-corrected area table, e.g. mrm_areas.csv, for shot-to-peak quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-area-integration--a6b1f2a7d6bda9dc))
- **peak-area-integration**: Use when GC × GC–MS deconvoluted features must be quantified across all samples — the annotated peaks from GcDUO's module III need area/intensity values, not just detection and annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-area-integration--6508ac4c357594f4))
- **peak-area-intensity-normalization**: Use when you have a targeted metabolomics peak area intensity table (samples in rows, compounds in columns) with assigned internal standards and you need to prepare data for quantification via regression models or statistical testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/peak-area-intensity-normalization))
- **peak-area-quantification**: Use when you have a referenced 2D 13C INADEQUATE spectrum and need to detect peaks and quantify their areas with a multi-threshold sweep, before network filtering and metabolite matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-area-quantification))
- **peak-area-to-concentration-conversion**: Use when the input is a lipidomic peak-area/peak-height matrix whose rows include internal standard species and a companion standards table (Standard/Class/Conc.) exists, and the goal is a per-species concentration matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-area-to-concentration-conversion))
- **peak-array-decoding**: Use when mzIdentML-referenced peak list files (mzData, mzXML, mzML, MGF, DTA, MS2, PKL) must be parsed in Java to decode per-spectrum m/z-intensity peak arrays, scan/precursor metadata, and cvParam entries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/peak-array-decoding))
- **peak-artifact-classification**: Use when you have raw mzML files plus a detected peak list (mz, rt columns, e.g. from XCMS) and need to separate genuine metabolite peaks from artifacts without large manually annotated training sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-artifact-classification))
- **peak-atlas-framework**: scATAC/multiome consensus peak-set methodology — multi-strategy calling, support voting, iterative-overlap merge, quantify-then-prune, tier, validate — with rare-cell-type protection as a first principle. Use when deciding how to build or evaluate a fixed-width peak set, or choosing strategies, thresholds, and filtration tradeoffs. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/peak-atlas-framework) · ★1)
- **peak-atlas-multiome**: Consensus scATAC peak atlas from true 10x Multiome (paired RNA+ATAC, same cells): peaks per RNA/ATAC/WNN/cell-type grouping, pseudo-replicate reproducibility, four-way vote, then Primary+Rescue quantify-and-prune protecting rare populations. Use when RNA+ATAC are paired. For unpaired data use peak-atlas-unpaired; builds on peak-atlas-framework. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/peak-atlas-multiome) · ★1)
- **peak-atlas-unpaired**: Consensus scATAC peak atlas from unpaired data (separate scRNA/scATAC pools): bridges modalities via gene-activity + staged CCA transfer, calls peaks (coarse/refined/label-free), tier-stratifies, gates risky peaks on external correlation. Use when RNA/ATAC aren't the same cells. For true multiome use peak-atlas-multiome. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/peak-atlas-unpaired) · ★1)
- **peak-background-binary-classification**: Use when you have LC-HRMS profile-mode chromatograms with extracted local maxima exported as standardized 2D rt×mz areas, and you need to disambiguate true chromatographic peaks from background signals (including wall artifacts and noise) at scale. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/peak-background-binary-classification))
- **peak-background-region-classification**: Use when ChIP-seq bins (e.g. 1000-bp from spikChIP/SeqCode binning) must be partitioned into peaks vs background regions to quantify how a normalization strategy (spikChIP, ChIP-Rx, Tag removal) affects enriched vs non-occupied loci. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/peak-background-region-classification))
- **peak-based-parameter-estimation**: Use when starting from raw untargeted metabolomics data (.mzML, .mzXML, or .CDF from qTOF, orbitrap, or FT-ICR instruments) and needing dataset-specific centWave peak-picking and XCMS grouping parameters before feature-table generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-based-parameter-estimation))
- **peak-border-center-estimation**: Use when you have LC-HRMS chromatograms in retention time × m/z matrix format and need to automatically localize chromatographic peak positions and extents prior to matching against a reference peak library or generating CNN training instances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/peak-border-center-estimation))
- **peak-boundary-definition-and-validation**: Use when after nontargeted peak detection has identified candidate peaks in LC-MS chromatograms, when you need to establish exact peak start/end retention times and extract peak-level metadata (intensity, width, shape) for downstream feature quality evaluation and annotation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/peak-boundary-definition-and-validation) · 2 copies)
- **peak-boundary-eic-extraction**: Use when comparing feature tables from LC-MS processing tools and you must judge whether extra reported features are genuine or low-quality, by recomputing a shape-fitness score on the EIC around each reported peak boundary. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-boundary-eic-extraction))
- **peak-boundary-identification-in-extracted-ion-data**: Use when after extracting ion chromatograms and ion mobilograms from raw diaPASEF or DIA data within specified m/z, retention time, and ion mobility windows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/peak-boundary-identification-in-extracted-ion-data) · 3 copies)
- **peak-boundary-localization**: Use when after you have (1) extracted regions of interest (ROIs) around candidate peaks in LC-MS data and (2) run those ROIs through a trained CNN-Transformer peak detection model that outputs both binary peak classifications and bounding box coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/peak-boundary-localization) · 2 copies)
- **peak-bounding-box-coverage-evaluation**: Use when an acquisition controller's simulated or real MS2 scan schedule (mzML with MS1/MS2 events) must be scored by how many chromatographic peaks it actually fragmented, rather than counting MS2 scans. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-bounding-box-coverage-evaluation))
- **peak-bounding-box-matching**: Use when you have an mzML run containing MS2 fragmentation events plus an exported MZmine2 picked-peak bounding-box list for a corresponding full-scan run, and need per-peak coverage flags to score a fragmentation controller. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/peak-bounding-box-matching))
- **peak-caller-algorithm-dispatch**: Use when a ChIPseq pipeline must call enrichment peaks from filtered .bam alignments and the peak calling algorithm and its parameters are configured in a centralized NEAT Targets.txt file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/peak-caller-algorithm-dispatch))
- **peak-caller-benchmark-comparison**: Use when benchmarking ChIP-Seq peak callers on one shared treated+control dataset (e.g., NRSF GSE13047 or STAT1) to compare peak counts and TF-motif enrichment across HPeak, PeakFinder, FindPeaks, MACS, SISSRs, CisGenome, and ChIPseeqer. - **peak-caller-benchmarking**: Use when multiple CC peak callers are available for qbed input and you must decide which caller to trust, or when reproducing the Pycallingcards Fig. 2c-e accuracy benchmark comparing callers on bulk and single-cell HCT116 datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/peak-caller-benchmarking))
- **peak-caller-method-comparison**: Use when comparing differential ChIP-Seq occupancy methods (e.g. GenoGAM vs csaw vs DESeq2) on the same dosage-responsive dataset using ROC/AUC against a quantile-defined gold standard. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/peak-caller-method-comparison))
- **peak-caller-scaling-visualization**: Use when comparing computational cost (wall-clock runtime, peak RSS memory) of calling-cards peak callers across a dilution series of qbed datasets with increasing insertion counts (e.g., &gt;5M insertions). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/peak-caller-scaling-visualization))

- **peak-collision-flagging**: Use when you have extracted a peak list from MSI data and need to annotate matrix-related signals, but overlapping peaks or isobaric ions (ions with identical or near-identical m/z values) risk being misclassified. - **peak-detection-mass-spectrometry**: Use when you have raw mass-spectrometry data files (mzML, mzXML, or vendor formats) from untargeted metabolomics experiments and need to extract differential metabolic ion peaks for downstream statistical or annotation analysis. - **peak-end-rule**: Activate when: user says 'how will they remember this,' 'experience design,' 'journey design,' 'memorable moment,' 'end-of-experience,' or 'our NPS is lower than expected'; when designing or auditing a multi-stage customer or user journey; when a competitor with similar quality earns higher recommendation rates. Do NOT activate when: the interaction is instantaneous with no temporal sequence (single API call, one-tap action); or when total real-time utility matters more than retrospective memory (welfare assessments, health measurements). More: deciqai.com/s/peak-end-rule ([deciqAI/knowledge-skills](https://github.com/deciqAI/knowledge-skills/tree/HEAD/peak-end-rule) · ★16)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-analysis-of-sc-rna-seq-pluripotency-scores))
- **pearson-correlation-and-rmsd-analysis**: Use when validating that automated RT assignments match manual RT assignments, e.g. comparing metScribeR-derived RTs against manually assigned MassHunter RTs on authentic metabolite standards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-and-rmsd-analysis))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmark-against-simulator-baselines**: Use when evaluating whether simulated nanopore reads (DNA metagenomic or direct-RNA) preserve known strain or transcript-isoform relative abundances and when comparing fidelity against other simulators. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmark-against-simulator-baselines))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmark-comparison**: Use when a trained TF-affinity model must be evaluated across experimental platforms (e.g. HT-SELEX training, uPBM testing) and ranked against published predictors via per-TF Pearson correlations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmark-comparison))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmark-computation**: Use when you have per-site methylation frequencies from a Nanopore caller (e.g., output.per_site.aggregated from DeepMod2) plus matched short-read WGBS or methylation microarray ground truth, and need a genome-wide correlation benchmark per sample and flowcell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmark-computation))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmark-computation**: Use when validating deconvolution output of bulk profiles into subpopulation-specific HiChIP/RNA-seq/ATAC-seq profiles by correlating predicted vs. true per-subpopulation profile vectors (e.g., in silico GM12878/K562 mixtures). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmark-computation))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when evaluating fine-tuned DNA foundation models on the DNALONGBENCH RSAP regression task and reporting per-sample PCC for Human, Mouse, and average against the expert-model benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmark-evaluation))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a regression model's continuous predictions (e.g. ΔΨ per variant) must be scored against held-out experimental values on an independent test set, as in CAGI-style blind challenges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmark-evaluation))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when validating a trajectory-inference method against ground-truth time points (e.g., annotated sampling days in a time-series CyTOF or scRNA-seq dataset) and you need a single quantitative score per method to compare against ablations and baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmark-evaluation))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmark-validation**: Use when a deconvolution method's predicted cell-type abundances must be benchmarked against known true abundances from aggregated single-cell data to quantify accuracy per cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmark-validation))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating predicted single-cell perturbation responses (e.g., control vs stimulated PBMC) against ground truth by DEG overlap and squared Pearson correlation per cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmarking--f3b2986132e2461b))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmarking**: Use when a model predicts per-cell malignant labels on many externally curated scRNA-seq datasets and you must verify that aggregated per-dataset malignant fractions match those reported by the source database (e.g., CancerSCEM) using Pearson correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmarking--d3e9414de2c011b9))
- **pearson-correlation-benchmarking-across-modalities**: Use when you have cell-level embeddings from multiple modalities (joint mvTCR, RNA-only, TCR-only) plus a continuous per-cell target such as pMHC multimer read counts, and need to quantify whether multimodality improves prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-benchmarking-across-modalities))
- **pearson-correlation-bioactivity-scoring**: Use when you have a bioassay-annotated feature quantification table (e.g. features_quantification_matrix_edited_bioactivity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-bioactivity-scoring))
- **pearson-correlation-cell-similarity**: Use when clustering scRNA-seq cells in scQA's CC stage and a cell-cell similarity graph is needed from the quantitative representation Q2; also as reference for choosing out-neighbor counts (0.1n vs 0.05n) by dataset size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-cell-similarity))
- **pearson-correlation-cell-type-matching**: Use when a multi-timepoint scRNA-seq analysis must identify cells at t1 that do not match any t0-annotated cell type, after CrossTimeAnnotation has assigned preliminary labels and new cell types may have emerged over time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-cell-type-matching))
- **pearson-correlation-clinical-age-analysis**: Use when you have per-read telomere length tables (sample.telomap.tsv) from telomap runs on patient PBL samples plus per-patient ages, and need to test whether mean telomere length correlates with age. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-clinical-age-analysis))
- **pearson-correlation-coefficient**: Probes the ability of an automated metric to correlate with human judgments of translation quality. Specifically, it tests how well a model's predicted segment-level scores align with Direct Assessment (DA) human scores across multiple language pairs. Use when the user has predictions and gold and needs to compute Pearson correlation coefficient. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pearson-correlation-coefficient) · ★3)
- **pearson-correlation-coefficient-computation**: Use when you have an expression matrix (e.g. Hu-cancer-RNASeq-dataset genes x samples) and need Pearson correlations of each feature with a selected feature, with or without two-group significance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-coefficient-computation))
- **pearson-correlation-coefficient-computation**: Use when after annotating mass-difference pairs with candidate adduct identities, compute correlations to test whether putative parent and adduct ion intensities co-vary across the imaging pixel grid. High positive correlation (with Bonferroni-corrected p-value &lt; 0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-coefficient-computation))
- **pearson-correlation-colocalization-analysis**: Use when you have Space Ranger-processed Visium spot-level expression for transcriptionally active regions (TARs/uTARs) plus known cell-type marker genes and need to test whether uTARs spatially colocalize with specific cell types across tissue sections. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-colocalization-analysis))
- **pearson-correlation-computation**: Use when you have mock-community amplicon data with known expected genome/ASV abundances (e.g., Zymo, ATCC 16S, Phylotag) and simulated or real observed counts, and need R2 agreement metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-computation))
- **pearson-correlation-computation**: Use when you need to verify that a targeted-lipidomics assay produces a linear response — e.g., after importing Skyline peak-area results for a transition list and fitting response-versus-amount regressions per lipid class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-computation))
- **pearson-correlation-computation**: Use when you have two per-droplet numeric metrics (e.g., DIEM score.debris and percent spliced reads) aligned on shared droplet/barcode identifiers and need to quantify their linear agreement, expecting a high positive R (~0.7-0.99). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-computation--75bb3a5e185e79a3))
- **pearson-correlation-computation**: Use when you have two condition-specific expression matrices (rows = gene symbols, columns = sample IDs) and need per-patient differential co-expression edges for downstream WMDS.net driver-node ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-computation--2125dbc3e5291a82))
- **pearson-correlation-computation**: Use when validating that CLEAR-derived FPB and CIRCscore values from ribo− paired-end RNA-seq reproduce published Pearson correlations against FPKM/FPM or RT-qPCR measurements. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-computation--4680dee8866dc7d3))
- **pearson-correlation-computation-across-tools**: Use when you have quantification results from two or more independent implementations, versions, or variants of the same analysis tool (e.g. C++ salmon 1.11. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/pearson-correlation-computation-across-tools))
- **pearson-correlation-cutoff-network-filtering**: Use when you have a PCC-scored functional association network (e.g. from FAVA on scRNA-seq or proteomics data) and need to extract a high-confidence subnetwork at STRING-style probability cutoffs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-cutoff-network-filtering))
- **pearson-correlation-deconvolution-assessment**: Use when simulated bulk HiChIP mixtures have known subpopulation-specific loop counts and you must quantify deconvolution accuracy or benchmark a model-based inference against a simple plug-in baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-deconvolution-assessment))
- **pearson-correlation-distance-computation**: Use when you need a continuous cell ordering (pseudo-time) from a binary/transformed eeSNV matrix: Pearson correlation distance between cells feeds a minimum spanning tree that must recapitulate a primary-to-metastasis trajectory and expose subgroup ramifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-distance-computation))
- **pearson-correlation-distance-preservation-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether a 2D embedding of high-dimensional single-cell (CyTOF/scRNA-seq) data retains global inter-cluster geometry, using manually annotated cell-population labels and centroid distance concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-distance-preservation-analysis))
- **pearson-correlation-expression-interaction-analysis**: Use when a cell×gene interaction-score matrix (e.g., MAPIT scores) is co-profiled with single-cell transcriptome data and you need per-gene correlation of binding strength with expression across subpopulations defined by cell cycle or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-expression-interaction-analysis))
- **pearson-correlation-feature-association**: Use when an untargeted lipidomics feature table has few biological groups (e.g. resistant vs parent strains, ~4 replicates each) and you need a feature-level association ranking to complement multivariate PCA/PLS-DA cluster separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-feature-association))
- **pearson-correlation-feature-collapsing**: Use when a two-condition metabolomics feature table contains redundant or highly correlated metabolites (e.g. |Pearson r| &gt; 0.7) or co-classed compounds (e.g. BCAAs, fatty acids) that should be collapsed into single nodes before joint network estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-feature-collapsing))
- **pearson-correlation-feature-mask-analysis**: Use when a registered multimodal MSI dataset has a thresholded binary pixel annotation (e.g. Ly6G IFM segmentation mask) and the goal is to identify the molecular signature — such as ether-linked PCs like PC O-36:4 — of a specific cell population. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-feature-mask-analysis))
- **pearson-correlation-feature-ranking**: Use when you have a trained VAE/latent-space MSI classifier (e.g. massNet Code layer output) plus decoded per-m/z spatial images and a pixel-level class-probability map, and need to identify which m/z peaks drive tumor delineation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-feature-ranking))
- **pearson-correlation-feature-transformation**: Use when preparing a scRNA-seq expression matrix (CSV) plus class labels for graph-embedding visualization with GraphDDP/graph_embed, after class filtering, normalization, and feature selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-feature-transformation))
- **pearson-correlation-gene-ranking**: Use when you have scPagwas per-cell gPAS scores for a GWAS trait and a normalized scRNA-seq matrix, and need to derive a ranked trait-relevant gene set (e.g., top 1,000 genes) for downstream enrichment or gene-set comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-gene-ranking))
- **pearson-correlation-gene-selection**: Use when imputing dropout-affected zero entries in an scRNA-seq expression matrix and you must choose which genes from a pre-defined gene subspace serve as regression predictors for a target gene. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-gene-selection))
- **pearson-correlation-grn-estimation**: Use when benchmarking a cell-specific network inference method against dyngen's per-cell ground-truth regulatory networks and you need a simple correlation-differencing baseline (SSN) using Pearson correlation as the base estimator. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-grn-estimation))
- **pearson-correlation-hierarchical-clustering**: Use when clustering a log2-transformed, median-centered gene expression matrix (samples × transcripts) with a Pearson-correlation-distance hierarchical method, and when you need stable cluster assignments across K values to compare against an alternative clustering method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-hierarchical-clustering))
- **pearson-correlation-hierarchical-clustering**: Use when you have deep features from a trained DeepCC model and need to test whether features self-organize into biological groups (e.g., three groups mapping to CMS1 MSI, CMS3 metabolic, CMS4 EMT). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-hierarchical-clustering))
- **pearson-correlation-imputation-evaluation**: Use when comparing gene imputation methods on paired observed/reference matrices (e.g. SERM vs MAGIC, SAVER, scVI on the cellular taxonomy dataset) and you need a quantitative accuracy score per sampling efficiency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-imputation-evaluation))
- **pearson-correlation-matrix-computation**: Use when a feature table of variant-prioritization predictor scores (e.g., the 574 dsQTL-positive and 27,735 negative SNPs of training dataset 1) must be screened for collinearity before feeding scores into ncDeepBrain or gene prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-matrix-computation))
- **pearson-correlation-matrix-computation**: Use when validating that a per-cell background/noise estimate (µ1) captures shared variance with isotype controls in CITE-seq ADT data, or when quantifying concordance of fitted parameters between alternative mixture-component definitions (k=2 vs k=3). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-matrix-computation--202e36b42f605555))
- **pearson-correlation-matrix-computation**: Use when a supervised cell annotation pipeline must decide between model architectures based on cross-cell-type similarity, or when a K x K cell-type correlation matrix from averaged scRNA-seq expression profiles is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-matrix-computation--61116901fd336189))
- **pearson-correlation-matrix-construction**: Use when a single-cell isoform-level count matrix is too sparse for reliable cell-level Pearson correlation, and a noise-robust, cell-type-summarized co-expression matrix is needed as input to isoform clustering (e.g. acorde semi-automated clustering). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-matrix-construction))
- **pearson-correlation-meta-analysis**: Use when the same feature/gene pairs have Pearson correlation coefficients measured across multiple independent studies (each with its own sample size) and they must be combined into a single estimate with fixed or random effects weighting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-meta-analysis))
- **pearson-correlation-method-comparison**: Use when multiple gene-set activity scoring methods (e.g. ORIGINS2 network activity, AUCell, mean expression) have been run on the same gene sets and you need to quantify their agreement with Pearson correlations and summary means. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-method-comparison))
- **pearson-correlation-model-comparison**: Use when comparing CNN, HyenaDNA, or Caduceus variants against an expert model on base-pair-resolution regression of transcription initiation signals over 100,000-bp sequences in DNALONGBENCH. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-model-comparison))
- **pearson-correlation-of-alignment-functions**: Use when comparing or validating the retention-time shift correction produced by MetMatch against an independent alignment (e.g., XCMS) on LC-HRMS feature lists, to confirm both tools recovered consistent non-linear Rt shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-alignment-functions))
- **pearson-correlation-of-count-matrices**: Use when two raw gene-barcode count matrices produced by different scRNA-seq pipelines on the same FASTQ dataset must be compared numerically to validate that an alternative pipeline reproduces native-pipeline output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-count-matrices))
- **pearson-correlation-of-cross-model-scores**: Use when two sequence-based variant-effect models (e.g. DeepFun and DeepSEA) have been run on the same SNP set and you must quantify per-assay score agreement, such as CTCF, DNase-seq, H3K4me3, or H3K9ac concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-cross-model-scores))
- **pearson-correlation-of-crup-probability-tracks**: Reference for computing or benchmarking the cor_CRUP generic feature (Pearson correlation between summed CRUP-EP and CRUP-PP probability tracks across cell types) when building or ablation-testing ET-interaction feature vectors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-crup-probability-tracks))
- **pearson-correlation-of-fold-change-signatures**: Use when two differential analysis results (e.g., young-vs-old log2 fold-change per ATAC-seq peak in men vs women) must be compared on a common feature set to measure how much of the response is shared versus sex- or condition-specific. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-fold-change-signatures))
- **pearson-correlation-of-genomic-tracks**: Use when you have CscoreTool-M cscore.txt or cscore.bedgraph sub-compartment probabilities and an independent track (NAD-seq, Lamin-B1 DamID) that can be aggregated over the same 100 kb windows, and you want to quantify agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-genomic-tracks))
- **pearson-correlation-of-pathway-activity-with-clinical-phenotype**: Use when you have a gene expression matrix paired with a continuous clinical variable (e.g. 1-hour OGTT blood glucose) and need to test which of ~400 predefined KEGG/Biocarta pathways' activity levels correlate with the phenotype. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-pathway-activity-with-clinical-phenotype))
- **pearson-correlation-of-peak-areas**: Use when validating a GC×GC–TOFMS peak-picking/quantification workflow against reference software using paired peak-area tables, especially with a serial dilution series (e.g. 1 ppm Breath Mix QC) where both correlation and dilution linearity must be shown. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-peak-areas))
- **pearson-correlation-of-peak-areas-to-mixing-ratios**: Use when evaluating metabolomics peak-area quantification accuracy on a dilution/mixing series with known expected ratios, especially comparing two processing tools' feature tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-peak-areas-to-mixing-ratios))
- **pearson-correlation-of-per-protein-background-levels**: Use when a CITE-seq (or ASAP-seq/TEA-seq) ADT dataset has both a raw empty-droplet matrix and a cell-containing matrix, and you need to verify that empty-droplet counts faithfully capture the ambient antibody background before using them for dsb step I correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-per-protein-background-levels))
- **pearson-correlation-of-per-site-methylation-levels**: Use when comparing methylation levels between a targeted/enrichment Nanopore run (e.g., RRMS adaptive sampling) and a whole-genome Nanopore run to quantify concordance of per-site CpG methylation frequencies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-per-site-methylation-levels))
- **pearson-correlation-of-perturbation-ranking-profiles**: Use when comparing perturbation effect ranking lists produced under alternative MUSIC preprocessing settings (imputation on/off, Cell_filtering on/off) to measure ranking-list similarity and identify which strategy shifts the ranking most. - **pediatric-brain-tumor-seg-eval**: Evaluates deep learning architectures for multi-class segmentation of pediatric brain tumors on MRI scans. It probes the model's ability to accurately delineate tumor sub-regions (whole tumor, enhanced tumor, cystic component, edema) and assesses cross-domain generalizability to adult glioma data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on PED BraTS 2024, CBTN, BraTS Adult Glioma 2023, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports lesion-wise Dice. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pediatric-brain-tumor-seg-eval) · ★3)
- **pediatric-brain-tumor-wsi-eval**: This benchmark evaluates the ability of weakly supervised multiple instance learning models to classify pediatric brain tumors from whole-slide histopathology images. It probes fine-grained diagnostic discrimination across varying class granularities (2 to 7 classes) under conditions of class imbalance and limited data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Pediatric brain tumor WSI dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Macro F1. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pediatric-brain-tumor-wsi-eval) · ★3)
- **pediatric-fever-triage**: Use when a caregiver reports a child (age 0 to under 18) with fever or subjective warmth at night, with or without accompanying symptoms. Produces a three-way next-action decision — Call (emergency or hotline), Go (open pharmacy), or Wait (home self-care) — with the Yorukusu safety boundary on every output. Inputs include age, temperature (measured or subjective), fever duration, concurrent symptoms, antipyretic history, and the caller's Personal Health Context. Never outputs a diagnosis and never recommends prescription medication. ([Guttyo/yorukusu](https://github.com/Guttyo/yorukusu/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pediatric-fever-triage) · ★1)
- **pediatric-growth-assessment**: Assesses pediatric growth using WHO and CDC growth charts with percentile and z-score calculations. Use when user asks to "plot growth", "check growth chart", "growth assessment", "growth percentiles", "is the child growing normally", "failure to thrive", "FTT workup", "pediatric BMI", mentions "growth velocity", "weight for age", "height for age", or needs growth trend analysis for a child. Do NOT use for adult BMI, adult weight management, or prenatal growth (use prenatal-visit-workflow). ([langcare/langcare-mcp-fhir](https://github.com/langcare/langcare-mcp-fhir/tree/HEAD/skills/core/specialty/pediatric-growth-assessment) · ★75)
- **pediatric-growth-reference-educator**: Explain the WHO Child Growth Standards and growth reference concepts for educational use. Educational and synthetic by design; outputs are drafts for review. ([salikkhann/openmedskills](https://github.com/salikkhann/openmedskills/tree/HEAD/dist/agent-skills/pediatric-growth-reference-educator) · ★5)
- **pediatric-obesity-consultation**: Guide a non-stigmatising pediatric obesity consultation using the 5As framework (Ask, Assess, Advise, Agree, Assist) and 4Ms assessment (Metabolic, Mechanical, Mental Health, Social Milieu), based on the CMAJ 2025 guideline. Trigger when a clinician asks how to approach a weight conversation with a child or family, how to use the 5As for obesity, how to assess a child with obesity, or how to conduct a non-judgmental obesity visit. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-consultation) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-etiology-screener**: Screen a child with obesity for red flags suggesting monogenic, syndromic, or secondary endocrine causes — distinguishing atypical from typical exogenous obesity. Trigger when a clinician asks whether obesity could be genetic or hormonal, suspects Prader-Willi syndrome, Cushing disease, hypothyroidism, ROHHAD, Bardet-Biedl syndrome, leptin deficiency, or craniopharyngioma, or when a child has early-onset severe obesity, short stature with obesity, hyperphagia, hypotonia, dysmorphic features, or developmental delay with weight gain. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-etiology-screener) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-intervention-selector**: Select the right treatment tier for a child with obesity — behavioural, pharmacologic, or surgical — using the CMAJ 2025 pediatric obesity guideline. Trigger when a clinician asks what to do for a child with obesity, which intervention to start, whether to escalate treatment, or when to consider medication or surgery for pediatric obesity. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-intervention-selector) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-selector**: Select the right pharmacologic agent for a child aged 12+ with obesity using the CMAJ 2025 guideline — choosing between GLP-1 receptor agonists, metformin, or orlistat with monitoring guidance. Trigger when a clinician asks which medication to use for pediatric obesity, whether to start semaglutide or metformin in a child, or how to manage obesity pharmacologically in adolescents. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-selector) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-surgical-screener**: Screen a child aged 13+ with obesity for eligibility for bariatric surgery (laparoscopic sleeve gastrectomy or Roux-en-Y gastric bypass) using the CMAJ 2025 guideline. Trigger when a clinician asks whether a child qualifies for weight loss surgery, bariatric surgery in adolescents, sleeve gastrectomy in a teenager, or when to refer for surgical management of pediatric obesity. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-surgical-screener) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-optometry**: Pediatric Visual Development & Amblyopia: Preferential looking tests (Teller acuity cards), cycloplegic refraction, strabismus detection, amblyopia patching regimens, and refractive error tracking. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/optometry/pediatric-optometry))
- **pediatric-ot**: Pediatric Occupational Therapy & Milestone Tracking: Peabody Developmental Motor Scales (PDMS-2), fine motor coordination, grasp pattern development, visual-motor integration (Beery VMI), and school-based OT intervention. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/occupational-therapy/pediatric-ot))
- **pediatric-speech**: Early Childhood Speech & Language Milestones: Late talker identification, parent-implemented language interventions (Hanen model), emergent literacy facilitation, and developmental sound acquisition tracking. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/speech-language-pathology/pediatric-speech))
- **pediatric-surgeon**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a pediatric general surgeon — triaging bilious vomiting or an acute abdomen in a neonate, deciding operative timing and staged-versus-definitive repair for congenital anomalies (malrotation, gastroschisis, CDH, biliary atresia), managing necrotizing enterocolitis by clinical stage, or working an acute scrotum against a salvage-time clock. US pediatric surgical practice (APSA guidelines). A reasoning aid, not medical advice. ([wonsukchoi/domain-experts](https://github.com/wonsukchoi/domain-experts/tree/HEAD/roles/pediatric-surgeon) · ★27)
- **pediatric_drug_safety**: Pediatric Drug Safety Review - Evaluate pediatric drug safety: pediatric use info, child safety, dosage forms, and overdosage info from FDA. Use this skill for pediatric pharmacology tasks involving get pediatric use info by drug name get child safety info by drug name get dosage forms and strengths by drug name get overdosage info by drug name. Combines 4 tools from 1 SCP server(s). ([SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw](https://github.com/SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pediatric_drug_safety) · ★399 · 4 copies)
- **pediatrician**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a general outpatient pediatrician — triaging a sick-child complaint by age and risk, structuring a well-child visit against the periodicity schedule, deciding whether a fever or growth-curve change warrants workup versus reassurance, or navigating a vaccine-hesitant parent conversation. US primary-care pediatrics (AAP/CDC guidelines). A reasoning aid, not medical advice. ([wonsukchoi/domain-experts](https://github.com/wonsukchoi/domain-experts/tree/HEAD/roles/pediatrician) · ★27)
- **pediatrics**: &gt; ([NeuralBlitz/Mito](https://github.com/NeuralBlitz/Mito/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/pediatrics) · 2 copies)
- **pediatrics-calculators**: Use when applying pediatric decision rules or growth/weight-based calculations. Includes PECARN head injury rule, CDC/WHO growth standards, weight-based dosing rules, Holliday-Segar maintenance fluids, and Parkland burn formula. ([rhavekost/clinical-toolkit](https://github.com/rhavekost/clinical-toolkit/tree/HEAD/dist/consumer/claude/pediatrics-calculators) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **pediatrics-neonatology**: Pediatric and neonatal clinical safety skill. Enforces strict weight-based dose caps (never exceeding maximum adult doses), flags age-weight entry discrepancies (lb vs kg), and screens for neonatal red flags. ([seotarek/MedOrqis](https://github.com/seotarek/MedOrqis/tree/HEAD/skills/pediatrics-neonatology) · ★1)
- **pedido**: Estrutura um pedido de programação do NOTAS PM em contexto, objetivo, escopo, forma de verificar e ação permitida, completando só o que faltar. Use quando o usuário digitar /pedido, ou quando o pedido for vago e de risco (exclusão, repasse, sincronização, banco, publicação em produção). ([cleitonjussara-coder/cleitonjussara-coder.io](https://github.com/cleitonjussara-coder/cleitonjussara-coder.io/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pedido) · ★1)
- **Pedido de Áudio**: Gera áudio TTS com conteúdo relevante em vez de repetir o pedido do usuário. ([rovanni/NewClaw](https://github.com/rovanni/NewClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/audio_request) · ★1)
- **pedido-afetacao-repetitivo**: Use para Pedido Afetacao Repetitivo em precedentes vinculantes, com análise de fatos, provas, teses, riscos, redação e resultado útil no Direito brasileiro. Não use para conclusão definitiva sem autos suficientes, fonte atual ou revisão profissional. ([bbpropulse/apsquad-dist](https://github.com/bbpropulse/apsquad-dist/tree/HEAD/skills/pedido-afetacao-repetitivo))
- **pedido-aplicacao-imediata-tese**: Use para Pedido Aplicacao Imediata Tese em precedentes vinculantes, com análise de fatos, provas, teses, riscos, redação e resultado útil no Direito brasileiro. Não use para conclusão definitiva sem autos suficientes, fonte atual ou revisão profissional. ([bbpropulse/apsquad-dist](https://github.com/bbpropulse/apsquad-dist/tree/HEAD/skills/pedido-aplicacao-imediata-tese))
- **pedido-claro**: Puente de entendimiento por pedido: antes de ejecutar un pedido ambiguo, refleja en una frase qué entendiste, pasa el texto por `spec_lint` si es un requisito, y haz solo las preguntas que cambian el resultado. Al cerrar, si el pedido era vago, ofrece una versión mejor formulada para la próxima vez. Es el hermano por-pedido de `discovery-intake` (que cubre el proyecto entero). ([contactandrewchl-wq/turtle-mcp](https://github.com/contactandrewchl-wq/turtle-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/pedido-claro) · ★2)
- **pedido-de-adiamento-de-julgamento**: Use para atuação de alta performance em Pedido de Adiamento de Julgamento, com admissibilidade, precedentes, persuasão, sessão, pedidos e estratégia posterior. Não use para decisão final, assinatura, protocolo ou citação não verificada. ([bbpropulse/apsquad-dist](https://github.com/bbpropulse/apsquad-dist/tree/HEAD/skills/pedido-de-adiamento-de-julgamento))
- **pedido-de-adjudicacao-alienacao-particular-ou-leilao**: Use para Pedido de Adjudicação, Alienação Particular ou Leilão no Direito brasileiro, com análise do título, prova, estratégia do credor e defesa do executado, pedidos e resultado útil. Não use para conclusão definitiva sem autos suficientes, fonte atual ou revisão profissional. ([bbpropulse/apsquad-dist](https://github.com/bbpropulse/apsquad-dist/tree/HEAD/skills/pedido-de-adjudicacao-alienacao-particular-ou-leilao))
- **pedido-de-admissao-como-amicus-em-precedente**: Use para elaborar, revisar ou impugnar Pedido de Admissão como Amicus Curiae em Precedente no processo civil brasileiro, com cabimento, provas, teses, pedidos e estratégia. Não use para decisão final, assinatura, protocolo ou citação não verificada. Não use para decisão final, assinatura, protocolo ou citação não verificada. Não use para decisão final, assinatura, protocolo ou citação não verificada.
